Human: {'role': 'user', 'content': [{'tool_use_id': 'toolu_014iA4Afz1jaAi5Df7jPSSoQ', 'type': 'tool_result', 'content': '560\t    def run_checks(self) -> list:\n561\t        """Run all HC-series system health checks. Returns list of results."""\n562\t        checks = [\n563\t            self.check_dependencies(),\n564\t            self.check_api_keys(),\n565\t            self.check_sqlite_integrity(),\n566\t            self.check_ports(),\n567\t            self.check_cost_cap(),\n568\t        ]\n569\t        for c in checks:\n570\t            icon = "✓" if c["status"] == "PASS" else ("⚠" if c["status"] == "WARN" else "✗")\n571\t            print(f"[HC] {icon} {c[\'name\']}: {c[\'status\']} — {c[\'detail\']}")\n572\t        return checks\n573\t\n574\t\n575\tdef main():\n576\t    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="MCC Self-Health Runner")\n577\t    parser.add_argument("--tab", help="Test only elements from this tab")\n578\t    parser.add_argument("--scheduled", action="store_true",\n579\t                        help="Only run if schedule config says it is due")'}]}

Full report: `docs/FULL_DIAGNOSTIC_2026-06-07.md`.\n\n**The two big findings that explain "fixes do nothing":**\n1. **Monaco loader script has no `defer`/`async`** (mission_control.html:1945) — it blocks the entire `<body>` (including button wiring) from loading until the cdnjs CDN responds. This is almost certainly why buttons appear dead.\n2. **Two+ `mcc_server.py` processes can bind port 8080 simultaneously** on Windows (Python\'s `HTTPServer` sets `SO_REUSEADDR`). Requests get randomly routed between old/stale and new code — so a fix can look like it "didn\'t work" because the test request hit the zombie process.\n\n**The Save button mystery is solved:** PART C proved the server save works perfectly (STATUS.md grew 34,261 → 34,333 bytes on direct API call).
